# OpenMotion · Product videos with your agent

[简体中文](README.zh-CN.md) · [Browse on the website](https://open-design.ai/templates/) · [Back to OpenDesign](../../README.md)

**Find the motion for your next product launch.**

Launch a product, demonstrate a feature, tell a brand story, or share a milestone. Choose a reference for the message you need to communicate, then let your agent adapt it with your copy, brand, screenshots, and footage.

## From reference to your video

1. **Choose a reference.** Select a cover to watch the full video, or open the template page to inspect its sequence, palette, and motion notes.
2. **Copy straight into your agent.** Use the copy icon on a card’s text block, paste into Codex or another connected agent, and add your brief, brand, and assets.
3. **Review, refine, and reuse.** Have your agent build from the guide, check the content and motion, refine the generated project, then export HTML and MP4.

Install [OpenMotion](https://releases.open-design.ai/open-motion/install.md) once, then copy the card briefs directly. You can also download a motion.md package for your agent to use as a guide when building an editable project.

## Selected references

**A motion template library built around AI100 products.** Each reference includes a video, motion notes, and a motion.md package to help you and your agent understand its style, pacing, and content changes. Pick a reference, copy the brief, and create with your own brand and assets, then keep editing and reusing the project.

If these templates sparked an idea or helped you create something, consider [starring OpenDesign ⭐](https://github.com/nexu-io/open-design). Thanks for your support.

Still looking for the right template? [Share the type, use case, or reference you have in mind](https://github.com/nexu-io/open-design/discussions/categories/ideas) to help the team keep growing the library.

## Download the guide collection

Build your own reference library by downloading every motion.md package together with an ordered catalog and agent briefs.

<details>
<summary>Show batch download instructions</summary>

Clone this repository and run the following from its root with Node.js 24:

```sh
node scripts/motion-templates/download.ts --locale en --output .tmp/motion-guides-en
```

The script downloads original guide packages, an ordered catalog, and per-reference agent briefs. It verifies published SHA-256 hashes and reuses verified files on later runs. Downloading requires no model account and starts no generation; watch reference videos through the individual card links.

[catalog.json](catalog.json) · [download.ts](../../scripts/motion-templates/download.ts)

</details>

## Sources and maintenance

Reference source information is preserved in the catalog. The collection pairs reference videos with adaptation notes so you and your agent can understand the approach and create with your own assets.

<details>
<summary>Catalog version and maintenance</summary>

Entries and package links come from the [published Motion catalog](https://r.open-design.ai/resources/1500c9af-186b-457f-b74d-a58113f657d8/channels/default/main.json), version 0.1.0-p0.20261111.2, checked 2026-11-13. [catalog.json](catalog.json) stores the selected order and reference frames.

After updating the catalog, run `node scripts/motion-templates/render.ts` to generate both READMEs; add `--check` to verify them. The selected order here is independent of the in-product library.

</details>
